### T8b fix round 1, 2026-10-24: the yard buildings and the trees of the nursery (branch `tb-t8b`; CPU only, software-GL preview)

**What the QA found (GPU round 1, `D:/git2/n4spb-work/tauride/T8b/qa-r1/findings.md`):** two majors, both against the user's air photo. (1) The classical buildings of the yard at the west end (U-blocks `w23002928` / `w23002936`, the pavilion `w23002941`) were still the generic city's 4-storey yellow blocks with red or grey roofs: the photo has low two-storey buildings with blue-green roofs round a courtyard and a brown-roofed barn. (2) The trees were thin 5 .. 8 m stems in even rows with flat yellow-green diamond buds (they read as umbrellas, from the air as an orchard grid) and none stood inside the houses: the photo has big dense leafless trees filling and ringing the houses.

**Both claims verified and fixed.**
* **The yard.** `data/nursery.js YARD_HOUSES` (new) holds the three blocks with their REAL OSM rings and `wings` (axis-aligned rectangles that tile the ring); `nursery.js yardHouseGeometry` draws the ring as walls 7.4 m (pavilion 6.6 m) high with a darker foot, two rows of windows and a door, and hipped roofs (slope 0.46 / 0.4, overhang 0.35 m, a wing's roof runs to the centre line of the wing it joins: valleys by the depth buffer) in two blue-green tones. One collider box per wing (9; the courtyard is open). Three more buildings of the photo's yard are claimed through `SHEDS`: the grey flat box = the office `w30324512` (5 m, flat dark roof), the **brown-roofed barn = the garage `w239884014`** (3.4 m, a gable of brown tiles; the OSM ring has a footprint after all), the little cube between the blocks `w259579860` (3 m). The garage ring was moved 0.2 m north of the office wall so that no two hidden faces are coplanar. The group `tauride-nursery` now has 9 ids (`CLAIMED_IDS` = `SHEDS` + `YARD_HOUSES`; the literal in `groups.js` is pinned equal by the test).
* **The trees.** New template (`treeGeometry`, ~130 triangles, 8 m): a tapering trunk, seven forked branches and eleven irregular grey-brown twig masses (an octahedron with every vertex pushed by the hash and turned at random: no two share a plane), two or three with a muted spring tint; per instance a height (7 .. 11 m, 5.8 .. 8.4 m inside the houses) and a width factor 0.9 .. 1.25. `TREE_ROWS` grew to 17 rows: the gaps (step 3.7), both edges of the middle lane (4.6), the north edge and the orchard, a new south row and **8 rows INSIDE the houses** (`inside: <house id>`: the row is exempt from that house's exclusion but the crown stays inside its walls; every other house, the buildings, the yard blocks, the tracks, the piles and the crates stay clear). 218 trees (cap 260), one instanced mesh.

**Numbers** (CPU): the nursery 20,626 -> **38,006 triangles** in 5 draws (trees 11,224 -> 27,904, buildings and details 518 -> 1,218; test budget 40 k / 6), `landmarksTauride` 758,672 -> **776,052** (BUDGET 835 k unchanged), 24 colliders (was 12), `city` -1,388 triangles and -56 colliders against the T8b base (the nine buildings it no longer draws), `environment` identical to the delivery (+186 / -8 / -5 against the base). `layout-golden` North regenerated: 9 leaves, all of them the sub-group (3 -> 9 members, bounds, the claim set); the strip golden identical.

**Strip unchanged** (`take.sh` / `compare.mjs` against `t8b-base`, `D:/git2/n4spb-work/tauride/t8b-fr1`): `layout-golden` strip identical, `check` of the strip 25 modules identical, strip streets / city / environment signatures identical, the `chk-*` lane green (`north-hooks-test` 212), North streets signature identical. The North environment snapshot against the clean export: **the same 43 changes, all inside the lot + 8 m** (`--allow 930,1116,-1826,-1594`: RESULT: OK); `car-keepout-sim --quick` OK (12 checks).

**Tests:** `nursery-test` **57 checks OK** (14 mutant lines; changed: 6 replaced buildings, the yard rings are the data's, 24 colliders, the walks are stopped by every building and every wing, 150 < trees <= 260 with the `inside` rows exempt only from their own house, 9 claimed ids, no unclaimed neighbour within 160 m claimed), `vegetation-test` 100, `ground-test` 86, `data-test` 141, `north-keepout-test --parallel --footprints` 18, `claims-draw-audit --strict`, `landmarks-groups-check` OK.

**Open for the GPU QA:** the look of the new crowns (lumpy polygonal masses: in the software preview they read as a dense grey-brown canopy from the air and as faceted blobs from the ground; the lighting of the unlit sides is the preview's), the yard blocks against the photo (colours of the walls `#d4d1c1`, roofs `#7ea39b`; the porch and the curved bay of the pavilion are rectangles with low hips), the cars and debris of the photo's yard are not drawn. **A second pose of the user** (`1362, 174.9, -1540.7, -556.6, -46.1`, "Image #9": the image did not reach this round) was added to `nursery-preview.mjs` as `user-shot-9`; read in the preview convention (yaw 0 = north, 90 = west) it looks south-south-west from the east of the garden and the lot is behind the camera (the preview draws 26 triangles): the QA should take the intended view from the user's HUD or screenshot.

### T8b fix round 2, 2026-10-24: the trees read as an orchard of umbrellas (branch `tb-t8b`; CPU only, software-GL preview in `D:/git2/n4spb-work/tauride/T8b/fr2/`)

**What the reviewers found (QA r2 + lens):** the round-1 trees were closer to the photo, but still stood in strict rows (step 3.7 .. 5.6 m, jitter 0.9 m) on 4.4 m bare trunks under faceted octahedron crowns with 2-3 saturated olive masses each: from the air an orchard grid, close up pyramids on poles. Verified in `04-p1-lowaerial.png`: true. **Fixed (data + template only; the strip, the claims and the hooks are untouched):** (1) the template is a LOW full crown (trunk 3.4 m of 8, was 4.4 m; boughs from 2.4 m; fourteen small masses on an egg-shaped crown, wide low and narrow on top, darker below and lighter above; ONE muted `#7c7b60` mass per tree, was two or three at `#8f9168` / `#979a6c`); (2) the rows are only a guide: `TREE.jitter` 0.9 -> 3.0 m, `TREE.skip` 0.16 (a hash empties a place of a row), `TREE.pair` 0.22 (a clump: a second tree 1.7 .. 2.8 m away, same clearance rules through one `free()` predicate), heights 6.2 .. 11.2 m (inside the houses 5.2 .. 8.6 m), width factor 0.78 .. 1.3, per-instance tint 0.86 .. 1.12 with a small warm / cool shift. **Numbers:** trees 192 (was 218), nursery 38,902 triangles (trees 28,800; budget 40 k), 5 draws, 24 colliders; `nursery-test` 57 OK, `vegetation-test` 100, `north-hooks-test` 212, `north-keepout-test` 18, layout-golden strip and North identical, `check` of landmarksTauride and `--area-modules` OK. **Open for the GPU QA:** the look of the clumps at the low-aerial / ribs poses; if the crowns still read as slabs, thin the masses (smaller radii, more of them) rather than touching the rows again.

### T8a, 2026-10-24: three fixes of the owner - the z-fight on the alley at the west gate, the transparent roof of the pavilion at the round plaza, the plate on Yesenin's pedestal (branch `tb-t8a` from `feat/areas` e019172; CPU only, software-GL stand-in frames; the committed bake of M8 goes stale on purpose, the lead rebakes)

**Commits** (every message ends with the Co-Authored-By line): the roof decks (`HOOKS.deckUnderside`), the alleys (`overlapLevels`), the plate and the play lifts, the tools; the docs commit that holds this entry. The extra aerial pose of the owner (1362, 174.9, -1540.7, -556.6, -46.1; the real photo `user-aerial-real-1362_174.9_-1540.7.png`) is a view of `t8a-preview.mjs` (`user-aerial-real`) and goes to the GPU QA as a shot.

**Bug 1 - the hatching on the alley at the west gate (pose 930.7, 1.5, -1411.1, -101.2, -22.4).**
* **Cause (found with rays, not guessed).** `tools/tauride/zfight-probe.mjs` builds environment + module + streets + city and casts rays from above over a box, listing every pair of opaque surfaces within `eps` of each other; at the west gate the only pair was `tauride-paths-ribbons` with ITSELF: two triangles of the one ribbon mesh at the SAME height over 11.5 m2 (x 929 .. 933.8, z -1411.8 .. -1409.5). The two ways are `w1051226582` (the south alley, 12 vertices, ends at (931.4, -1411.8)) and `w81524425` (the main alley from the gate): the first ends ON the second without a shared vertex (no junction, no disc, no end fade) and runs with it for 5 m. Both are class `main`, so the draping gave them the same height (mesh + 3 cm + 4 mm), and the shader's tonal bands (dark centre line, light edges, rim: functions of each ribbon's own across-coordinate) fought pixel by pixel: the faint rectangular patch of diagonal hatching. The street pavement, the apron slab, the kerb skirt, the gate stub, the fence plinth, the terrain mesh and the environment ground were all ruled out by the same probe (the pairs at the gate: floor over the environment ground by 5 mm at the outline = the T3 design; `pt-gutter` over `pt-asphalt` by 1 cm = the streets', as in the strip; the tree blobs are decals).
* **Not only the west gate.** `t8a-test` section 1 samples the planned ribbons: 479 m2 in the garden where two different ways are within 0.1 mm in height (the mutant, `levelStep` 0): the west gate 11.5 m2, the SOUTH gate (n828488069) 25.5 m2 (w1051226582 x w419263230), the gate n4222941897 4.0 m2, and ~440 m2 of fans and Ts inside the garden. The other 8 gate zones had none of this kind.
* **Fix (`paths-draped.js`).** `overlapLevels(pos, idx, wayOf, ways, P)`: the ground is cut into cells of 0.5 m, two ways of one class that share a cell are neighbours, and the ways are coloured greedily (a way that ENDS on another goes first = lowest, then narrow first, then by index: deterministic) so that neighbours never share a LEVEL; the vertices of a way at level k are lifted by k x 0.4 mm (`PATHS_DRAPED.levelStep`, `levelMax` 4 = 1.6 mm, under the 2 mm between the classes: main over side over trail is kept). 5 levels are used, 0 overflow, 106 neighbour pairs; 0 .. 1.6 mm of height, the triangle count unchanged (11,084), the draping within 5 cm of mesh + 3 cm + bias + level on every sampled vertex. Where the through way lies over a stub the stub's bands would have drawn a seam: a way end that lies on another way (`endOnWay`: within half the other's width + the pad of its polyline) is now a junction end for the shader's fade (`vPath.y`), and the rim darkening fades with it (program key `tauride-paths-t8a`). Remaining coplanar area in the garden: 11.5 m2 of crossing lines of two ribbons that meet at an angle (a line, not a patch), none in any gate zone.
* **The same artefact where it was NOT between ribbons (found by the 11-gate ray scan).** The football pitch's turf (3.2 cm) and the play surfaces (3.5 cm) lie over the alley ribbons (3.0 .. 3.6 cm) at the four pitch gates and in one playground: 0 .. 3 mm apart (z-fight at 45 m and beyond). `data/furniture-design.js`: `PLAY.pitchSurface.lift` 0.032 -> 0.046 and `PLAY.lift` 0.035 -> 0.046 (the alleys are then hidden under the turf inside the ring, 1 cm). The first run of the test with the old play lift had 593 rays within 3 mm: red; with both lifts: 0.
* **Proof.** `node tools/area.mjs north tools/tauride/t8a-test.mjs` section 1: the mutant (`levelStep` 0) has the west gate pair over 11.5 m2, the fix 0.0; all 11 gate zones at most 0.5 m2; the whole garden under a tenth of the mutant's; the levels within the limit; the draping and the determinism; `1b` (real meshes: environment + module + streets + city, 6,875 rays of 1 m over the 11 gate zones of 24 x 24 m): no two ribbon triangles in one plane (0.1 mm) on more than 3 rays per gate (worst 0), no other opaque surface of the module within 1 cm of another (the floor over the ground at the outline excepted), and the ribbons against the play surfaces over the pitch, the 3 playgrounds and the fitness pad (13,920 rays): none within 8 mm.
* **Frames (software GL through the engine's real render pipeline, no GPU: `t8a-preview.mjs`, `D:/git2/n4spb-work/tauride/T8a/preview/`).** `bug1-west-gate` (the owner's pose), `-2`, `-3`, `-low`, `bug1-south-gate`, `-2`: no hatching; what remains is a faint tone seam where the through way's darker centre line meets the stub's end (the shader's design: bands). The GPU frame is the QA agent's.

**Bug 2 - the transparent top of the pavilion w420650670 (pose 1100.4, 1.5, -1444.7, -36.2, 11.5).**
* **Cause.** NOT a clockwise cap (the T3 class): the 6 triangles of its deck at y 6.0 all face up (`addCap` winds every triangle by its own cross product). The deck is a ONE-SIDED face (`city/roofs.js addDeck`, `city-roof` material FrontSide) and the engine's normal / depth pre-pass is DOUBLE-SIDED (`core/renderer.js` ndMat `side: DoubleSide`): a viewer who stands below the eaves of a low building (the pavilion's cornice is 6 m up, the garden's walkers pass 1 m from it) sees the sky through the roof beyond the cornice's lip, and the outline pass draws the deck's edges (the thin dark polygon in the air). The user's own screenshot is exactly that; the strip has the same archetype, but its buildings are in streets where the eaves hide the deck from below, so nothing in the strip changes.
* **Measured.** From below (a ray going up from y 0.4 over a 0.7 m lattice, only faces that look at the ray count): **0 of 217 samples** of the pavilion and 0 of all 13 garden footprints (3,834 samples, w1308718054 / 55, w30198529 / 30, w30324534 / 35 / 36, w420650670, w423708264, w423966114, w1458068095 / 18 / 19) have a roof; from above all have (the decks at y 6.0 / 6.99 / 7.5 / 10.0, all facing up: no clockwise deck among them).
* **Fix (a strip-neutral hook, not a garden-local copy).** The sixteenth key `HOOKS.deckUnderside` (AREAS 9.1): `city/roofs.js addDeck` asks `HOOKS.deckUnderside(poly)` (through `lib/area-tables.js`; the strip's HOOKS is `{}`: the call is a falsy test) and for a yes adds the SAME cap facing down, 1 cm lower, in 0.6 of the roof colour (a soffit). `src/areas/north/hooks/decks.js makeDeckUnderside`: true for a deck whose first vertex is a vertex of one of the 13 footprints of `data/footprints.js` or whose centre lies in the garden outline; `undefined` unless `MODULES` has `landmarksTauride`. +70 triangles in `city-roofs`, nothing else; `roofmap` (an ortho depth pass for the highest surface) and the colliders do not see it. The owner's constraint (a fix in the city must not change the strip) is met by the hook, and a garden-local copy of the city's building would have lost the facade grammar.
* **Proof.** `t8a-test` section 2: the strip's `src/world/area.js` has `HOOKS = {}` and no key; `addDeck` WITHOUT the key is byte-identical to the old `addCap` (positions, normals, indices); WITH it the top is as before and the underside is the same triangles, facing down, 1 cm lower, darker, `B.cur` restored; the real city: 13 of 13 footprints have a roof from above AND from below (217 / 217 for the pavilion); the MUTANT (the city built with the key deleted): the pavilion 0 / 217 from below, 217 / 217 from above. `north-hooks-test` knows the 16 keys (211 checks).
* **Frames.** `bug2-user-pose` (the owner's pose), `bug2-corner`, `-north`, `-east`, `-south`, `-west`, `bug2-aerial` (25 m): the dark olive soffit under the eaves, no sky through the roof, the deck from above unchanged.

**Bug 3 - the plate 'Сергей Есенин' on the pedestal of n4222941889 (pose 1005.6, 1.5, -1359.9, -229.2, 0.7).**
* `monuments.js`: `plateSpec(m, D)` (pure: the text, the size, the local frame, the world normal `(sin rotY, 0, cos rotY)`: the face the pedestal looks at, the alley), `plateGeometry(g, spec)` (the painted front quad and the four edges of a raised plate: 10 triangles), `paintPlate(g, w, h, plate)` (a dark bronze field with a rim and a thin inner line, the text in gold with a dark shadow and a light edge, fitted to 86 % of the width), and `buildMonuments` makes ONE mesh `tauride-yesenin-plate` with one canvas texture of 512 x 132 px (`ctx.tex.draw`, key `tauride-plate-yesenin`, painted at load: nothing is baked, no new bake dependency). Data: `data/furniture-design.js YESENIN.plate` (text, 0.86 x 0.22 m, 8 mm proud, colours). The plate is on the FRONT face of the shaft (the face toward the nearest alley, local +z), centred, y 0.33 .. 0.55 on the shaft 0.14 .. 0.74: 8 mm in front of the face (a raised plate: the 8 mm edge is real geometry, no coplanar face, no z-fight), colour layer only (no outline), no shadow cast.
* **Cost.** +1 draw (the monuments site 10 -> 11 draws), +10 triangles (`check:north` landmarksTauride 738,046 -> 738,056, budget 815 k), one 512 x 132 canvas (67,584 px).
* **Proof.** `t8a-test` section 3: the text is exactly 13 code points `421 435 440 433 435 439 20 415 441 435 43d 438 43d` (no mojibake; the data file's bytes are `d0 a1 d0 b5 ...`); the plan has the plate on Yesenin's pedestal and not on Chaikovsky's; its normal faces the alley point the pedestal looks at (0.00 degrees, the alley point 19.1 m away; the test bound is 5); the plate lies on the shaft (inside its width and height, above the base, under the cornice) 8 mm proud; the built geometry has 10 triangles and the front quad's normal from its winding is the plan's within 1 degree and 8 mm in front of the face; `paintPlate` on a recording 2d context draws exactly the string (3 passes); `buildMonuments` makes exactly one plate mesh with a canvas map; the MUTANT (a plan without the plate) builds none and one draw fewer.
* **At the owner's pose the plate is NOT in the frame - say it plainly.** The pose stands 1.1 m from the pedestal's centre at eye height 1.5 m, pitch +0.7: the plate (y 0.33 .. 0.55) is 1 m below the axis of the view and about 0.6 m in front of it, i.e. 60 degrees below the frame's lower edge (the vertical field is +-31). ### T8a fix round 1, 2026-10-24 (branch `tb-t8a`; no GPU)

* **Fixed (lens, major): `npm run hooks:all` was red.** The 16th HOOKS key `deckUnderside` was added to `tools/north-hooks-test.mjs` but not to `CONTRACT_KEYS` of `tools/north-keepout-test.mjs` (two failing checks: the key list of `makeHooks(MODULES)` and the `dormant` / `real` / `free` hook lists of the variants). `CONTRACT_KEYS` now has the sixteen keys (the title says so), `npm run hooks:all` is green: hooks:syntax OK (21 files), north-hooks-test 211, height 324, keepout 18, people 11; the command is in the T8a checklist of section 10. Lesson: a new HOOKS key means two test files (north-hooks-test KEYS and north-keepout-test CONTRACT_KEYS), not one.
* **Not changed: the plate at the owner's exact pose (qa, major).** Verified by the geometry: the pose is 1.1 m from the centre of a 1.0 m pedestal, i.e. 0.6 m from its face at eye height 1.5; the plate (y 0.33 .. 0.55) lies 1 m below the axis, about 60 degrees under a frame that ends at -31. Any plate on the front of this 0.6 m shaft is out of that frame; only a plinth of about 1.3 m or more (the T6 height that T7 slimmed because it dominated the figure) or a plate carried by the figure's own feet could be in it. That is a design decision (the monument's proportions against a pose that stands against it): reported to the lead, the plate stays on the shaft where it reads at 3 m and 6 m (day-25 / day-26) and from the side. The frame the owner posted still shows only the coat, trousers and shoes.
* **Not changed: the aerial comparison (qa, major).** The eight differences (stair landing, sports centre, pitch, water colour, bridge and path colours, tree habit, people) are design data of T3-T7 (`landmarksTauride` ground, paths, monuments, bridges, vegetation), not of the three fixes of T8a; they are listed for the lead as the brief of the next design phase (the landing as a D-shaped terrace with a wide fan of steps is the largest).
* **Qa-gate blocker (signatures not run): re-run and green.** `take.sh strip-light north-light` on this tree against `m6-baselines` (`compare.mjs --only strip-`): `strip-golden-diff` identical, `strip-check` identical in 25 modules, `strip-streets-signature`, `strip-city-signature` and `strip-environment-regress` identical (+0); `north-golden-diff` exit 0. ### T8a fix round 2, 2026-10-24 (branch `tb-t8a`; no GPU; no `src/` file changed)

* **Verified, not changed: the plate at the owner's exact pose (lens major, qa major, qa-gate blocker).** Re-derived from the data (pedestal n4222941889 at 1004.7, -1359.3, rotY 2.240; the plate's normal (0.784, -0.620); the pose 1005.6, -1359.9): the camera stands 1.08 m from the centre, 0.578 m from the face, exactly on the plate's normal; with the vertical half field of 30 .. 31 degrees and pitch +0.7 the lower edge of the frame hits the face at y 1.14 .. 1.16, and the plate (y 0.33 .. 0.55, centre 0.44) is 61.4 degrees below the axis, i.e. 30 degrees under the frame. The claim of the reviewers is right and it is NOT a code defect: no plate on the front of a 0.6 m shaft (shaft 0.14 .. 0.74) can be in that frame, and neither can anything on the slab (its near half is under the frame too: the feet are at 880 of 900 px). The plate stays where it reads (day-14 pitch -42, day-15 at 3 m, day-16 at 6 m, night-02: gold on bronze, not mirrored).
* **Decision for the lead (not mine):** (a) answer the owner that the plate is on the front face below eye level (look down, or step back 2 m); or (b) make the plate reachable from his exact frame: the plate must lie at y >= 1.2 (centre ~1.3), i.e. `YESENIN.pedestal.h` about 1.4 (shaft 0.14 .. 1.54) and `plate` moved up on the shaft (plateSpec reads the shaft; the plate y is derived from the shaft, so the recipe is one number plus the plate row), the figure's feet then at 1.54: total 1.54 + 1.96 = 3.5 m against the OSM 3.1 m, 3.29 m with `figure.scale` 1.0. This is the T6 proportion (1.5 m shaft) that T7 slimmed because it dominated the 1.75 m figure at 20 m; (a) keeps T7's look. Re-run `t8a-test` section 3, `furniture-test` and `check:north` after the change (the collider, the photo spot and the monuments budget read the same row).
* **Not changed: the aerial comparison (qa major, the owner's second shot 1362, 174.9, -1540.7, -556.6, -46.1).** The layout matches (the pitch, the canal bend, the island, the bridges, the landing, the sports centre, the street); the eight differences ranked by the qa (the D-shaped landing with a fan of ~8 steps, the sports centre's pale hipped roof with a drum cupola, the dark turf with stands, near-black smooth water, grey flat bridges, pale gravel paths, few big irregular trees on dry meadows, people and a cart) are design data of T3-T7 (ground, paths, monuments, bridges, vegetation, the water shader), not of the three fixes of T8a. The pose is a preview view (`t8a-preview.mjs user-aerial-real`) and a GPU shot (`std-05-u-aerial-real.png`); the list is the brief of the next design phase, the landing first.
* **Strip untouched:** no `src/`, `tools/` file changed in this round (the strip oracles of round 1 stand: layout-golden strip identical, `strip-check` 25 modules identical, the streets / city / environment signatures +0). The bake is as stale as before.

The lot read as a brown field with grey-brown bare crowns next to the green garden. **Fixed (colours only; layout, glasshouses, buildings, colliders, tracks, counts untouched):** the floor shader (`nursery.js` BODY, `data/nursery.js FLOOR_COLORS`) now paints the garden's own lawn (the same hex as `ground-material.js GROUND_COLORS` uGA / uGB / uGC / uClover) with clover patches and light flecks, the lanes between the houses lush herb green, the beds under the glass low green crops with taller herbs, the far mean colour green; the tracks stay pale light-brown gravel (`#c3b394` / `#b2a283`, pebble flecks `uPeb`), the fenced yard stays pale gravel.
